05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/app_api/models/travlr.js
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const { toDecimalPrice, toPriceNumber, canonicalPrice, PRICE_MESSAGE } = require('../utils/price');

// Define the trip schema.
const tripSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
  code: { type: String, required: true, index: true },
  name: { type: String, required: true, index: true },
  length: { type: String, required: true },
  start: { type: Date, required: true },
  resort: { type: String, required: true },
  // CS 499, Alondra Paulino Santos, 2026-09-17: exact decimal USD storage.
  perPerson: {
    type: mongoose.Schema.Types.Decimal128,
    required: true,
    set: toDecimalPrice,
    validate: {
      validator(value) {
        try { canonicalPrice(value); return true; } catch { return false; }
      },
      message: PRICE_MESSAGE
    }
  },
  image: { type: String, required: true },
  description: { type: String, required: true }
});

// Keep Decimal128's BSON wrapper out of the API. Clients receive a JSON number.
// The explicit price limit keeps this conversion safe to the nearest cent.
tripSchema.set('toJSON', {
  transform(doc, result) {
    result.perPerson = toPriceNumber(doc.perPerson);
    return result;
  }
});

const Trip = mongoose.model('trips', tripSchema);
module.exports = Trip;
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/app_api/models/user.js
const crypto = require('crypto');
const jwt = require('jsonwebtoken');
const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const userSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
  email: {
    type: String,
    unique: true,
    required: true,
    lowercase: true,
    trim: true
  },
  name: { type: String, required: true, trim: true },
  // Public registration creates customers; only a deliberate staff grant can
  // enable administrative APIs. Missing roles on older accounts are customers.
  role: { type: String, enum: ['customer', 'staff'], default: 'customer' },
  hash: { type: String, required: true },
  salt: { type: String, required: true }
});

userSchema.methods.setPassword = function(password) {
  this.salt = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
  this.hash = crypto
    .pbkdf2Sync(password, this.salt, 1000, 64, 'sha512')
    .toString('hex');
};

userSchema.methods.validPassword = function(password) {
  const hash = crypto
    .pbkdf2Sync(password, this.salt, 1000, 64, 'sha512')
    .toString('hex');
  return this.hash === hash;
};

userSchema.methods.generateJwt = function() {
  return jwt.sign(
    {
      _id: this._id,
      email: this.email,
      name: this.name,
      role: this.role || 'customer'
    },
    process.env.JWT_SECRET,
    { expiresIn: '1h' }
  );
};

module.exports = mongoose.model('User', userSchema);

05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/app_api/utils/price.js
/**
 * CS 499 price enhancement for Alondra Paulino Santos | 2026-09-17.
 * Store exact USD decimal values in MongoDB; expose numbers to the UI.
 * Validate instead of silently rounding, coercing blanks, or losing cents.
 */
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const MAX_PRICE = 999999999.99;
const PRICE_MESSAGE = 'Price must be a number from 0 to 999999999.99 with at most two decimal places.';

function canonicalPrice(value) {
  if (value instanceof mongoose.Types.Decimal128) value = value.toString();
  if (typeof value !== 'number' && typeof value !== 'string') {
    throw new TypeError(PRICE_MESSAGE);
  }
  const text = String(value).trim();
  // Plain dollar amounts only: currency symbols and grouped numbers need review.
  if (!/^\d+(?:\.\d{1,2})?$/.test(text) || Number(text) > MAX_PRICE) {
    throw new TypeError(PRICE_MESSAGE);
  }
  const [whole, fraction = ''] = text.split('.');
  return `${BigInt(whole)}.${fraction.padEnd(2, '0')}`;
}

function toDecimalPrice(value) {
  return mongoose.Types.Decimal128.fromString(canonicalPrice(value));
}

function toPriceNumber(value) {
  return Number(canonicalPrice(value));
}

// The public API contract requires JSON numbers, not numeric-looking strings.
function isApiPrice(value) {
  if (typeof value !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value)) return false;
  try { canonicalPrice(value); return true; } catch { return false; }
}

module.exports = { MAX_PRICE, PRICE_MESSAGE, canonicalPrice, toDecimalPrice, toPriceNumber, isApiPrice };
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/app_api/utils/trip-search.js
/**
 * CS 499 | Alondra Paulino Santos | 2026-09-17
 * Shared search contract: validate scalar URL values before building a query.
 * Clients cannot supply database operators, field names or regular expressions.
 */
const { canonicalPrice, toDecimalPrice } = require('./price');
const MAX_SEARCH_LENGTH = 100;
const DEFAULT_SORT = 'departureAsc';
const SORT_OPTIONS = Object.freeze([
  { value: 'departureAsc', label: 'Departure: earliest first', order: { start: 1, _id: 1 } },
  { value: 'departureDesc', label: 'Departure: latest first', order: { start: -1, _id: 1 } },
  { value: 'priceAsc', label: 'Price: low to high', order: { perPerson: 1, _id: 1 } },
  { value: 'priceDesc', label: 'Price: high to low', order: { perPerson: -1, _id: 1 } },
  { value: 'nameAsc', label: 'Trip name: A to Z', order: { name: 1, _id: 1 } }
]);
const FIELDS = ['q', 'minPrice', 'maxPrice', 'startDate', 'endDate', 'sort'];

class SearchValidationError extends Error {
  constructor(errors, criteria) {
    super('Please correct the search fields.');
    this.name = 'SearchValidationError';
    this.errors = errors;
    this.criteria = criteria;
  }
}

/** Parse a real YYYY-MM-DD calendar date, rejecting JavaScript date rollover. */
function parseDate(value) {
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value) || value.startsWith('0000')) return null;
  const date = new Date(`${value}T00:00:00.000Z`);
  return Number.isFinite(date.getTime()) && date.toISOString().slice(0, 10) === value ? date : null;
}

/** Escape metacharacters so user text is a literal substring, not a pattern. */
function escapeRegex(value) {
  return value.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
}

/**
 * Build a validated MongoDB search from Express query parameters.
 * Empty fields mean no filter; price bounds are inclusive Decimal128 amounts.
 * Date bounds are UTC calendar days. The exclusive next-day end includes the
 * entire selected day without depending on the server's local time zone.
 * @param {Object} input Parsed URL parameters, never a MongoDB query.
 * @returns {{criteria: Object, filter: Object, sort: Object}}
 * @throws {SearchValidationError} Field errors and bounded values for the form.
 */
function buildTripSearch(input = {}) {
  const errors = {};
  const criteria = {};
  for (const name of Object.keys(input)) {
    if (!FIELDS.includes(name)) errors.query = 'Remove unsupported search parameters and try again.';
  }
  for (const name of FIELDS) {
    const raw = input[name];
    if (raw !== undefined && typeof raw !== 'string') errors[name] = 'Provide one value for this field.';
    criteria[name] = typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.trim() : '';
  }
  if (!criteria.sort) criteria.sort = DEFAULT_SORT;
  if (criteria.q.length > MAX_SEARCH_LENGTH) errors.q = `Use no more than ${MAX_SEARCH_LENGTH} characters.`;
  const selectedSort = SORT_OPTIONS.find(option => option.value === criteria.sort);
  if (!selectedSort) errors.sort = 'Choose one of the listed sorting options.';
  const prices = {};
  for (const name of ['minPrice', 'maxPrice']) {
    if (!criteria[name]) continue;
    try { prices[name] = canonicalPrice(criteria[name]); }
    catch { errors[name] = 'Enter a price from 0 to 999999999.99 with at most two decimal places.'; }
  }
  if (prices.minPrice && prices.maxPrice && Number(prices.minPrice) > Number(prices.maxPrice)) {
    errors.maxPrice = 'Maximum price must be at least the minimum price.';
  }
  const dates = {};
  for (const name of ['startDate', 'endDate']) {
    if (!criteria[name]) continue;
    dates[name] = parseDate(criteria[name]);
    if (!dates[name]) errors[name] = 'Enter a real calendar date in YYYY-MM-DD format.';
  }
  if (dates.startDate && dates.endDate && dates.startDate > dates.endDate) errors.endDate = 'End date must be on or after the start date.';
  if (Object.keys(errors).length) {
    for (const name of FIELDS) criteria[name] = criteria[name].slice(0, MAX_SEARCH_LENGTH);
    throw new SearchValidationError(errors, criteria);
  }
  const filter = {};
  if (criteria.q) {
    const literal = new RegExp(escapeRegex(criteria.q), 'i');
    filter.$or = [{ name: literal }, { resort: literal }];
  }
  if (prices.minPrice || prices.maxPrice) {
    filter.perPerson = {};
    if (prices.minPrice) filter.perPerson.$gte = toDecimalPrice(prices.minPrice);
    if (prices.maxPrice) filter.perPerson.$lte = toDecimalPrice(prices.maxPrice);
  }
  if (dates.startDate || dates.endDate) {
    filter.start = {};
    if (dates.startDate) filter.start.$gte = dates.startDate;
    if (dates.endDate) {
      const nextDay = new Date(dates.endDate);
      nextDay.setUTCDate(nextDay.getUTCDate() + 1);
      filter.start.$lt = nextDay;
    }
  }
  // Top-level filters are ANDed; only trip-name/resort matching is ORed.
  // The unique ID breaks ties to keep repeated requests in a stable order.
  return { criteria, filter, sort: { ...selectedSort.order } };
}

module.exports = { buildTripSearch, SearchValidationError, SORT_OPTIONS, DEFAULT_SORT, MAX_SEARCH_LENGTH };
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/app_server/controllers/inquiry.js
/**
 * Trip inquiries | CS 499, Alondra Paulino Santos | 2026-09-17.
 * Public form: show a real trip, validate and save a question, then redirect
 * so refreshing the confirmation page cannot submit the message twice.
 */
const jwt = require('jsonwebtoken');
const Trip = require('../../app_api/models/travlr');
const Inquiry = require('../../app_api/models/inquiry');
const { toPriceNumber } = require('../../app_api/utils/price');
const { formatPrice } = require('../utils/price-display');
const { validateInquiry } = require('../utils/inquiry-validation');

const emptyValues = { fullName: '', email: '', travelerCount: '', message: '' };
const safeTripCode = code => /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,40}$/.test(code);
const inquiryPath = code => `/travel/${encodeURIComponent(code)}/inquiry`;

function formToken(tripCode) {
  return jwt.sign({ purpose: 'trip-inquiry', tripCode }, process.env.JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: '30m' });
}

function validToken(token, tripCode) {
  if (typeof token !== 'string') return false;
  try {
    const claims = jwt.verify(token, process.env.JWT_SECRET, { algorithms: ['HS256'] });
    return claims.purpose === 'trip-inquiry' && claims.tripCode === tripCode;
  } catch { return false; }
}

/** A short-lived receipt keeps the success page tied to a completed save. */
function receiptToken(inquiry) {
  return jwt.sign({ purpose: 'inquiry-receipt', tripCode: inquiry.tripCode, inquiryId: String(inquiry._id) }, process.env.JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: '1h' });
}

function receiptClaims(token, tripCode) {
  if (typeof token !== 'string') return false;
  try {
    const claims = jwt.verify(token, process.env.JWT_SECRET, { algorithms: ['HS256'] });
    return claims.purpose === 'inquiry-receipt' && claims.tripCode === tripCode && /^[a-f0-9]{24}$/.test(claims.inquiryId) ? claims : null;
  } catch { return null; }
}

async function findTrip(code) {
  if (!safeTripCode(code)) return null;
  return Trip.findOne({ code }).maxTimeMS(3000).exec();
}

function formModel(trip, values = emptyValues, errors = {}, message = '') {
  return {
    title: `Ask About ${trip.name} - Travlr Getaways`,
    trip: { code: trip.code, name: trip.name, resort: trip.resort, priceDisplay: formatPrice(toPriceNumber(trip.perPerson)) },
    values, errors, message, token: formToken(trip.code)
  };
}

/** A missing trip is a normal 404; database failures get a generic 503. */
async function showForm(req, res) {
  try {
    const trip = await findTrip(req.params.tripCode);
    if (!trip) return res.status(404).render('inquiry-unavailable', { title: 'Trip Not Found', message: 'This trip is not available.' });
    return res.status(200).render('inquiry', formModel(trip));
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[inquiry] Trip lookup failed', { name: error.name });
    return res.status(503).render('inquiry-unavailable', { title: 'Unable to Load Trip', message: 'Unable to load this trip. Please try again.' });
  }
}

async function submitForm(req, res) {
  const code = req.params.tripCode;
  if (!safeTripCode(code)) return res.status(404).render('inquiry-unavailable', { title: 'Trip Not Found', message: 'This trip is not available.' });
  try {
    const trip = await findTrip(code);
    if (!trip) return res.status(404).render('inquiry-unavailable', { title: 'Trip Not Found', message: 'This trip is not available.' });
    if (!validToken(req.body?.formToken, code)) {
      const { values } = validateInquiry(req.body);
      return res.status(403).render('inquiry', formModel(trip, values, {}, 'Your form expired. Please try again.'));
    }
    const { values, errors } = validateInquiry(req.body);
    if (Object.keys(errors).length) {
      return res.status(400).render('inquiry', formModel(trip, values, errors, 'Please correct the fields below.'));
    }
    // The trip association and workflow status come from trusted server data.
    const inquiry = await Inquiry.create({
      tripCode: trip.code,
      tripName: trip.name,
      fullName: values.fullName,
      email: values.email,
      travelerCount: Number(values.travelerCount),
      message: values.message
    });
    return res.redirect(303, `${inquiryPath(code)}/sent?receipt=${encodeURIComponent(receiptToken(inquiry))}`);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[inquiry] Submission failed', { name: error.name });
    return res.status(503).render('inquiry-unavailable', { title: 'Unable to Send Inquiry', message: 'Unable to send your inquiry. Please try again.' });
  }
}

async function confirmation(req, res) {
  const claims = receiptClaims(req.query.receipt, req.params.tripCode);
  if (!claims) {
    return res.status(404).render('inquiry-unavailable', { title: 'Confirmation Unavailable', message: 'This confirmation link is unavailable.' });
  }
  try {
    const inquiry = await Inquiry.findOne({ _id: claims.inquiryId, tripCode: req.params.tripCode })
      .select('tripName referenceNumber').maxTimeMS(3000).lean().exec();
    if (!inquiry) return res.status(404).render('inquiry-unavailable', { title: 'Confirmation Unavailable', message: 'This confirmation link is unavailable.' });
    return res.status(200).render('inquiry-sent', {
      title: 'Inquiry Received - Travlr Getaways',
      tripName: inquiry.tripName,
      referenceNumber: inquiry.referenceNumber
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[inquiry] Confirmation lookup failed', { name: error.name });
    return res.status(503).render('inquiry-unavailable', { title: 'Unable to Load Trip', message: 'Unable to load this trip. Please try again.' });
  }
}

module.exports = { showForm, submitForm, confirmation };
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/app_server/controllers/main.js

    res.render('index', { title: 'Travlr Getaways' });
};

module.exports = {
    index
};
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/app_server/controllers/travel.js
/**
 * Public trip browsing | CS 499, Alondra Paulino Santos | 2026-09-17.
 * Preserve validated GET form values, call the API, and format display values.
 * Database access stays in the API; this controller owns page presentation.
 */
const { formatPrice } = require('../utils/price-display');
const { safeTripDescription } = require('../utils/trip-description');
const { buildTripSearch, SearchValidationError, SORT_OPTIONS } = require('../../app_api/utils/trip-search');
const dateFormat = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { dateStyle: 'medium', timeZone: 'UTC' });

/** Build only the form/display fields needed by the Handlebars template. */
function viewModel(criteria, errors = {}) {
    return {
        title: 'Find a Trip - Travlr Getaways', criteria, errors, trips: [],
        sortOptions: SORT_OPTIONS.map(({ value, label }) => ({ value, label, selected: criteria.sort === value }))
    };
}

/**
 * GET /travel: 400 renders field errors; 503 renders a safe retrieval error.
 * Successful empty results remain 200 and offer a way to clear the filters.
 */
const travel = async (req, res) => {
    let criteria;
    try {
        ({ criteria } = buildTripSearch(req.query));
    } catch (error) {
        if (!(error instanceof SearchValidationError)) throw error;
        return res.status(400).render('travel', {
            ...viewModel(error.criteria, error.errors), errorMessage: error.message
        });
    }
    const model = viewModel(criteria);
    try {
        // Trusted server configuration only: never use a user-supplied host/URL.
        const endpoint = new URL(process.env.TRIPS_API_URL || `http://127.0.0.1:${process.env.PORT || 3000}/api/trips`);
        for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(criteria)) {
            if (value) endpoint.searchParams.set(key, value);
        }
        const response = await fetch(endpoint, {
            headers: { Accept: 'application/json' }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000)
        });
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Trip API status ${response.status}`);
        const trips = await response.json();
        if (!Array.isArray(trips)) throw new Error('Unexpected trip response format');
        model.trips = trips.map(trip => ({
            ...trip, priceDisplay: formatPrice(trip.perPerson),
            // The template intentionally renders only this sanitized HTML.
            description: safeTripDescription(trip.description),
            // Use the same UTC calendar-day convention as the date filters.
            departureDisplay: dateFormat.format(new Date(trip.start))
        }));
        model.resultCount = trips.length;
        model.resultLabel = trips.length === 1 ? 'trip found' : 'trips found';
        model.noResults = trips.length === 0;
        return res.status(200).render('travel', model);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('[travel-page] Trip retrieval failed', { name: error.name });
        return res.status(503).render('travel', {
            ...model, errorMessage: 'Unable to retrieve trips. Please try again.'
        });
    }
};

module.exports = {
    travel
};

05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/app_server/utils/inquiry-validation.js
/**
 * CS 499 | Alondra Paulino Santos | 2026-09-17
 * Validate form data on the server. Browser attributes help users but can be
 * bypassed, so only these normalized values are saved to the database.
 */
const EMAIL_PATTERN = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

function validateInquiry(body = {}) {
  const errors = {};
  const values = {};
  for (const field of ['fullName', 'email', 'message']) {
    const input = body[field];
    if (typeof input !== 'string') errors[field] = 'Enter one value for this field.';
    values[field] = typeof input === 'string' ? input.trim() : '';
  }
  if (!errors.fullName && (values.fullName.length < 2 || values.fullName.length > 100 || /[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/.test(values.fullName))) {
    errors.fullName = 'Enter a name between 2 and 100 characters.';
  }
  if (!errors.email && (values.email.length > 254 || !EMAIL_PATTERN.test(values.email))) {
    errors.email = 'Enter a valid email address.';
  }
  const travelerInput = body.travelerCount;
  if (typeof travelerInput !== 'string' || !/^(?:[1-9]|1\d|20)$/.test(travelerInput.trim())) {
    errors.travelerCount = 'Enter a whole number of travelers from 1 to 20.';
  }
  values.travelerCount = typeof travelerInput === 'string' ? travelerInput.trim().slice(0, 2) : '';
  if (!errors.message && (values.message.length < 10 || values.message.length > 2000 || /[\x00-\x08\x0b-\x1f\x7f]/.test(values.message))) {
    errors.message = 'Enter a message between 10 and 2000 characters.';
  }
  // Bound any reflected values, including invalid submissions, before display.
  return {
    values: { fullName: values.fullName.slice(0, 100), email: values.email.slice(0, 254), travelerCount: values.travelerCount, message: values.message.slice(0, 2000) },
    errors
  };
}

module.exports = { validateInquiry };
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/app_server/utils/price-display.js
/** CS 499 | Alondra Paulino Santos | 2026-09-17: format numeric USD for display. */
const { isApiPrice } = require('../../app_api/utils/price');
const currency = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', { style: 'currency', currency: 'USD' });
function formatPrice(value) {
  return isApiPrice(value) ? currency.format(value) : 'Price unavailable';
}
module.exports = { formatPrice };
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/app_server/utils/trip-description.js
/**
 * Trip description presentation | CS 499, Alondra Paulino Santos | 2026-09-19.
 * Legacy descriptions contain paragraph markup. Sanitize that stored HTML at
 * the presentation boundary so formatting is retained without trusting data
 * merely because it came from the database or administrator application.
 */
const sanitizeHtml = require('sanitize-html');

function safeTripDescription(value) {
  if (typeof value !== 'string') return '';
  return sanitizeHtml(value, {
    allowedTags: ['p'],
    allowedAttributes: {},
    disallowedTagsMode: 'discard'
  });
}

module.exports = { safeTripDescription };

05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/bin/www
#!/usr/bin/env node

/**
 * Module dependencies.
 */

var app = require('../app');
var debug = require('debug')('cs465-fullstack:server');
var http = require('http');

/**
 * Get port from environment and store in Express.
 */

var port = normalizePort(process.env.PORT || '3000');
app.set('port', port);

/**
 * Create HTTP server.
 */

var server = http.createServer(app);

/**
 * Listen on provided port, on all network interfaces.
 */

server.listen(port);
server.on('error', onError);
server.on('listening', onListening);

/**
 * Normalize a port into a number, string, or false.
 */

function normalizePort(val) {
  var port = parseInt(val, 10);

  if (isNaN(port)) {
    // named pipe
    return val;
  }

  if (port >= 0) {
    // port number
    return port;
  }

  return false;
}

/**
 * Event listener for HTTP server "error" event.
 */

function onError(error) {
  if (error.syscall !== 'listen') {
    throw error;
  }

  var bind = typeof port === 'string'
    ? 'Pipe ' + port
    : 'Port ' + port;

  // handle specific listen errors with friendly messages
  switch (error.code) {
    case 'EACCES':
      console.error(bind + ' requires elevated privileges');
      process.exit(1);
      break;
    case 'EADDRINUSE':
      console.error(bind + ' is already in use');
      process.exit(1);
      break;
    default:
      throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Event listener for HTTP server "listening" event.
 */

function onListening() {
  var addr = server.address();
  var bind = typeof addr === 'string'
    ? 'pipe ' + addr
    : 'port ' + addr.port;
  debug('Listening on ' + bind);
}

05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/docs/ADMIN_INQUIRY_ENHANCEMENT.md
# Administrator inquiry management

Alondra Paulino Santos — CS 499, Software Engineering and Design  
Implemented: September 17, 2026

## Purpose

The customer inquiry form now has a staff workflow. Authorized staff can open
the Angular admin application's **Inquiries** page, review questions, filter
them by status, move through pages of 20, and change each status to New,
In progress, or Closed. A status changes only after **Save status** is pressed.
Each item includes its customer-facing reference number and traveler count so
staff can identify the request and understand the requested party size.
The inbox does not send email, reserve travel, or delete customer inquiries.

## How the code is organized

- `app_api/models/inquiry.js` holds the saved question, trip link, timestamps,
  and status. It includes indexes for the staff list and trip lookup.
- `app_api/controllers/inquiries.js` supplies a paginated, filtered list and
  validates status updates. It accepts only an explicit status field, preventing
  a request from changing a customer's name, email, or message.
- `app_api/middleware/staff-only.js` checks the user's **current role in the
  database** after JWT authentication. Removing staff access takes effect
  immediately, even when a previously issued token has not expired.
- `app_api/models/user.js` gives public registrations the customer role.
  The token includes a role for admin navigation, but the token claim alone
  never grants access to an API. Existing users without a role are customers.
- The trip create, edit, and delete APIs now use the same staff check. The
  previous token-only rule allowed any registered user to change trips.
- `app_admin/src/app/inquiry-list/` contains the inbox screen; its service
  and model are in `app_admin/src/app/services/inquiry-data.ts` and
  `app_admin/src/app/models/inquiry.ts`. Angular escapes inquiry text, and
  `white-space: pre-wrap` preserves line breaks without interpreting HTML.
- The inbox route, navigation link, trip editing buttons, and login redirect
  follow the staff role. Server checks remain authoritative.

## Granting access to the right account

Staff access is intentionally not assigned by public registration. First,
register or confirm the account to use in the enhanced database. Then run the
provisioning script from the enhanced project folder with that exact email:

```sh
npm run grant:staff -- person@example.com
npm run grant:staff -- person@example.com --apply
```

The first command is a dry run. The second grants staff access only to that
existing account in `travlr_capstone` (or the explicitly configured `DB_NAME`).
The script refuses the original `travlr` database. Log out and sign in again
after the role changes so the Angular menu receives a fresh role claim. The
API always checks the database role, including while an old token remains.

The enhanced database contained one existing account, `admin@travlr.com`.
That account was granted staff access for this implementation. No account in
the original CS 465 database was changed. If another account should become
staff later, use the dry run and apply commands above for its exact email.

## Verification

```sh
npm test
npm run test:staff
npm run test:inquiry
npm run test:search
npm run test:integration
cd app_admin
NG_BUILD_MAX_WORKERS=1 npm test -- --watch=false
NG_BUILD_MAX_WORKERS=1 npm run build -- --progress=false
```

The staff integration test uses a uniquely named disposable database and
checks guest rejection, customer rejection, staff access, status filtering,
pagination, invalid updates, public registration, and immediate revocation.
The inquiry and price integration checks continue to pass with staff-only
trip writes. The Angular unit suite and production build pass. The build
reports a nonblocking initial-bundle size warning; this enhancement does not
claim to solve the inherited overall bundle-size issue.

## Remaining work for the capstone

This completes the three planned customer and staff features in the CS 465
enhancement. The original CS 465 folder and database remain untouched. Before
submitting the milestone, review the screen while signed in, update the
software design document and narrative,
and package the original and enhanced artifacts according to the rubric.
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/docs/FINAL_IMPLEMENTATION_AUDIT.md
# CS 465 Capstone Enhancement: Final Implementation Audit

**Author:** Alondra Paulino Santos  
**Reviewed:** September 19, 2026

## Scope and alignment

The enhanced copy preserves the original CS 465 trip administration workflow:
staff authentication, trip listing, and authenticated create, update, and delete
operations. It adds the three approved software-engineering enhancements:

1. Public trip search, combined filters, and allowlisted sorting.
2. A trip-specific customer inquiry form with traveler count and a customer-facing
   reference number.
3. A staff-only inquiry inbox with status filtering, pagination, and explicit
   status updates.

Trip prices were also migrated from formatted strings to MongoDB `Decimal128` so
database comparisons and sorting use numeric values without losing cents.

## Design and code-quality review

- Routes, controllers, utilities, Mongoose models, Angular services, and UI
  components have separate responsibilities.
- Enhanced files use short headers and decision-focused comments. Comments explain
  validation, authorization, numeric storage, safe rendering, and workflow choices
  without repeating self-explanatory statements.
- API inputs use allowlists, bounded values, Mongoose validation, and generic
  failure messages. Raw database errors are logged as limited metadata instead of
  being returned to a browser.
- Public registration cannot assign the `staff` role. Protected endpoints validate
  the JWT and then check the user's current database role, so revoking staff access
  takes effect even when an older token still exists.
- The inquiry POST verifies a short-lived token and a real trip, applies server-side
  field validation, saves trusted server-owned fields, and uses a `303` redirect to
  prevent accidental resubmission on refresh.
- Legacy trip descriptions retain their paragraph formatting but are sanitized
  before unescaped rendering. Executable tags and attributes are removed.
- Maintained production dependencies were updated and `npm audit --omit=dev`
  reports zero known vulnerabilities.

## Verification completed

- Backend unit tests: **15 passed**.
- Price and original trip CRUD integration test: **passed**.
- Search/filter integration test: **passed**.
- Public inquiry and receipt integration test: **passed**.
- Staff authorization and inquiry-management integration test: **passed**.
- Angular component/service tests: **16 passed across 8 test files**.
- Angular TypeScript compilation (`tsc --noEmit`): **passed**.

The integration scripts create randomly named disposable test databases and remove
them afterward. They do not write to the original CS 465 database or the enhanced
application database.
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/docs/PRICE_ENHANCEMENT.md
# CS 499 - Numeric trip prices

Project: CS465-Capstone-Enhanced  
Student: Alondra Paulino Santos  
Implementation date: September 17, 2026

## What changed

Previously, `perPerson` was stored as text. The enhanced model stores a MongoDB
**Decimal128 number**, which preserves decimal dollar/cents values and supports
numeric database comparisons. The API returns an ordinary JSON number; Angular's
trip models and add/edit forms now use numbers as well. No booking, payment,
search, inquiry or staff-management features were added in this step.

Price rules are enforced in both the API and the Angular forms:

- Required, finite numeric value between $0 and $999,999,999.99.
- At most two decimal places; invalid precision is rejected, not rounded.
- Zero is allowed. Blank values, negative amounts, booleans, numeric strings in
  API requests, and currency symbols are rejected.
- The upper limit is an explicit application guardrail that keeps JSON-number
  conversion reliable to the nearest cent. It is not a MongoDB storage limit.

The public travel page now displays prices with a dollar sign and two decimal
places. The Angular trip cards retain their existing USD currency formatting.
Formatting does not change the stored amount. A JSON number such as `799` is
displayed as `$799.00`; JSON numbers do not preserve trailing zeros.

MongoDB tools may show `{"$numberDecimal":"799.00"}`. That is Extended JSON's
representation of a BSON decimal number, not a return to String storage.
Do future monetary calculations in decimal arithmetic or integer cents rather
than assuming JavaScript floating-point arithmetic is exact.

## Database isolation and conversion

- Original project database: `travlr` (not modified).
- Enhanced project database: `travlr_capstone` (the enhanced app's new default).
- All four source trips were copied; all four prices were converted and verified:
  $799.00, $1,199.00, $1,999.00 and $3,499.00.
- Existing local accounts and indexes were copied so the existing login works.
  Changes in the enhanced database do not change the original accounts or trips.
- An original trip-data backup is in `backups/original-trip-prices-*.json`.
  Backups are excluded from Git. Do not publish database credentials or accounts.

`DB_NAME` can override the enhanced database name. Do not point this enhanced
application at the original `travlr` database. Keep the two servers from running
on the same port simultaneously.

The conversion has already been performed on this computer. **Do not rerun the
seed script to migrate prices:** it deletes existing trips before inserting the
three sample records, which would lose the fourth trip.

For a fresh installation only, with the original local database present:

```sh
npm run prepare:prices
npm run prepare:prices -- --apply
```

The first command is a dry run. The second validates all source prices, saves a
trip-data backup, copies trips/accounts/indexes, and verifies the stored amounts.
It refuses an existing/nonempty target database or a target named `travlr`.
Malformed legacy amounts stop conversion before any database writes; they are not
guessed or rounded. If copying is interrupted, inspect the partial target rather
than rerunning a seed or deleting data without review.

To return to the original application, run the untouched original project, which
still uses its original database. Do not overwrite either copy as a rollback.

## Run the enhanced application

From `CS465-Capstone-Enhanced`, with local MongoDB running:

```sh
npm start
```

In a second terminal, from `CS465-Capstone-Enhanced/app_admin`:

```sh
npm start
```

The public travel page is at `http://localhost:3000/travel`; Angular is at
`http://localhost:4200`. Existing local credentials can be used in the enhanced
copy. This step does not add staff roles or change the authentication design.

## Verification completed

| Check | Result |
| --- | --- |
| Server price/model/serialization/display tests | 6 passed |
| Angular tests, including typed add/edit prices and currency display | 14 passed |
| Local API + MongoDB integration smoke test | Passed |
| Production Angular build | Passed; nonblocking initial-bundle size warning |

The integration test creates a uniquely named temporary test database and removes
only that test database afterward. It verifies create, retrieve, edit and delete;
rejection of invalid prices; BSON Decimal128 storage; zero-dollar values; numeric
sorting/filtering; and the public page's currency display. It does not write test
records into `travlr` or `travlr_capstone`.

Re-run from the enhanced project root:

```sh
npm test
npm run test:integration
```

From `app_admin`:

```sh
npm test -- --watch=false
npm run build
```

The integration test needs a local MongoDB server. Tests of the public page use
an isolated local API connection. A full manual browser walkthrough remains useful
when implementing the upcoming search and inquiry features.

## Main files to study

- `app_api/utils/price.js`: validation, exact decimal conversion, API numeric values.
- `app_api/models/travlr.js`: Decimal128 field and JSON serialization.
- `app_api/controllers/trips.js`: server-side create/update price checks.
- `app_admin/src/app/validators/price.ts`: matching form rules.
- Add/edit forms and both TypeScript trip models: numeric controls and types.
- `app_server/utils/price-display.js`, travel controller/template: USD display.
- `scripts/prepare-price-database.js`: safe, one-time copy/conversion.

Design reference: MongoDB's [monetary-data modeling guidance](https://www.mongodb.com/docs/v8.0/tutorial/model-monetary-data/)
describes Decimal128 as an exact decimal representation for monetary values.
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/docs/TRIP_INQUIRY_ENHANCEMENT.md
# Trip inquiry form

Alondra Paulino Santos — CS 499, Software Engineering and Design  
Implemented: September 17, 2026

## What changed

Each trip on `/travel` now links to `/travel/:tripCode/inquiry`. The page shows
the selected trip and asks for a customer's name, email address, number of
travelers, and question.
The form is an inquiry only; it does not reserve a trip or collect payment.

The server checks that the trip exists, validates the form, saves the inquiry
in the enhanced project's MongoDB database, and redirects to a confirmation
page with a short-lived signed receipt. A page refresh after submission
therefore does not post the question again, and opening the confirmation URL
without a receipt does not falsely report a successful submission. The
confirmation displays a unique, customer-friendly reference number and says
the question was saved for review; the project
does not send an email or claim that staff already answered it.

## Code and design decisions

- `app_server/views/travel.hbs` adds the link from each result.
- `app_server/views/inquiry.hbs`, `inquiry-sent.hbs`, and
  `inquiry-unavailable.hbs` show the form, confirmation and safe errors.
- `app_server/controllers/inquiry.js` handles GET and POST, looks up the trip,
  signs a 30-minute form token bound to its code, and redirects after a save.
  The token prevents a different website from submitting the browser form
  without first obtaining a valid token for the selected trip. It is not an
  anti-spam or rate-limiting system. A one-hour signed receipt protects the
  confirmation page from being opened as if a save had occurred.
- `app_server/utils/inquiry-validation.js` trims fields and validates them on
  the server. Name is 2–100 characters, email at most 254 with a basic email
  shape, traveler count a whole number from 1–20, and message 10–2000
  characters. The browser constraints are guidance;
  the server decides what is accepted. Invalid fields are shown by name, and
  bounded values remain in the form so the customer can correct them.
- `app_api/models/inquiry.js` stores a generated reference number, trip code,
  a snapshot of the trip name, normalized contact information, traveler count,
  message, creation time, and status `new`. Customers cannot choose the
  reference number or set a workflow status in the form.
- The reference follows `INQ-YYYYMMDD-XXXXXXXX`. It uses cryptographically
  random bytes and a unique database index, is separate from MongoDB's internal
  identifier, and is shown only after the signed receipt is verified against a
  saved inquiry.
- The templates escape user-provided values. Failed lookups or writes show
  generic customer-facing messages, without raw database errors.
- The navigation links are root-relative so they work from the nested inquiry
  URL. The CSS stays scoped to the travel and inquiry pages.

This public form writes to the enhanced database (`travlr_capstone` by default).
It does not modify the original CS 465 application or its database. Staff
inquiry management, authorization, notifications, and production spam controls
remain separate enhancements.

## How to verify

With local MongoDB available, run these commands in the enhanced project:

```sh
npm test
npm run test:inquiry
npm run test:search
npm run test:integration
npm start
```

Then open `http://localhost:3000/travel`, choose **Ask about this trip**, and
review the form. Submitting a real question creates a record in the enhanced
database; the automated inquiry test instead creates and removes a uniquely
named temporary database. Its checks cover the linked form, price display,
invalid fields, invalid traveler counts, invalid token, unknown trip, saved
values, generated reference, default status, and confirmation redirect.
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/docs/TRIP_SEARCH_ENHANCEMENT.md
# Trip search and filters

Alondra Paulino Santos — CS 499, Software Engineering and Design  
Implemented and verified: September 17, 2026

## Purpose and scope

Customers previously had to browse every trip to find a suitable destination.
The public `/travel` page now searches trip names or resorts, combines minimum
and maximum prices with departure-date filters, and sorts the matching trips.
The existing page theme and API array response are preserved.

This enhancement does not implement the planned inquiry form or administrator
inquiry management. It does not modify the original CS 465 project or its database.

## How the implementation works

1. The customer submits the labeled GET form. Its URL can be bookmarked.
2. A shared server-side validator checks the search fields. Invalid fields return
   HTTP 400 with an explanation; the form retains bounded input values.
3. The API builds its own MongoDB query from the validated values. Name/resort
   matching is OR; the active text, price and date filters combine with AND.
4. MongoDB filters and sorts before returning records. Prices use the exact
   Decimal128 storage introduced in the price enhancement, not string sorting.
5. The public controller formats prices and departure dates and renders results.
   No matches returns HTTP 200 with a clear-filters option, not a database error.
6. Retrieval failures return HTTP 503 with a generic customer-facing message.

## Input contract and design decisions

- `q`: optional literal substring of trip name or resort, case-insensitive,
  trimmed, maximum 100 characters. Regex metacharacters are escaped.
- `minPrice`, `maxPrice`: optional inclusive USD bounds from 0 to 999999999.99,
  at most two decimal places. Zero is a valid bound. Minimum cannot exceed maximum.
- `startDate`, `endDate`: optional real calendar dates in YYYY-MM-DD format.
  Both selected days are included. The database query uses UTC midnight for the
  start and excludes midnight immediately after the end day. This includes late
  departures on the end day and avoids dependence on the server's local time zone.
- `sort`: `departureAsc` (default), `departureDesc`, `priceAsc`, `priceDesc`, or
  `nameAsc`. A unique `_id` tie breaker makes identical primary values stable.
- Unknown parameters, repeated values, arrays, objects and unlisted sort choices
  are rejected. Clients cannot select arbitrary database fields or operators.
- Existing past-dated sample trips remain visible unless the customer filters
  them out. The enhancement does not silently change the sample data.
- The public controller uses a trusted server-configured API URL, never a host
  provided by a customer. Its request timeout is five seconds; the MongoDB query
  has a three-second execution limit. These are distinct limits, not a guarantee
  that every type of database connection failure ends within three seconds.
- Handlebars escapes search values. Validation errors are associated with their
  labeled fields, and controls have visible keyboard focus styles.

## Reading order for the code

1. `app_api/utils/trip-search.js`: shared validation, query construction and sorts.
2. `app_api/controllers/trips.js`: list endpoint, database query and response status.
3. `app_server/controllers/travel.js`: retained form values, API call and formatting.
4. `app_server/views/travel.hbs`: search form, field errors and trip results.
5. `public/css/trip-search.css`: styles scoped to the search feature.

Comments document intent, boundaries and non-obvious decisions rather than
repeating every statement. Price conversion remains documented separately in
`docs/PRICE_ENHANCEMENT.md`.

## Verification

Run from the enhanced project folder with local MongoDB running:

```sh
npm test
npm run test:integration
npm run test:search
npm start
```

Open `http://localhost:3000/travel`. A different API location can be configured
on the server with `TRIPS_API_URL`; otherwise the page calls the local API on
`PORT` (default 3000).

Verified results:

- All 11 unit tests passed, including five search-contract tests.
- The existing price integration checks passed after the search changes.
- Search integration checks passed for combined filters, case-insensitive literal
  text, numeric ordering, zero-price bounds, inclusive end dates, rejected invalid
  input, retained form values, empty results, HTML escaping and safe failure messages.
- Integration scripts use uniquely named temporary databases and remove only
  those databases after the checks. They do not write application data.
- Browser verification: searching `reef` with maximum price `1000` displayed
  one matching trip, Gale Reef Updated at $799.00, and retained both filters.
- The search form was visually checked against the existing desktop page design.

## Limits and future work

The current small catalog returns all matching records without pagination.
Unanchored case-insensitive substring searches may scan a large collection;
this work does not claim an indexed search-performance improvement. A larger
catalog would require measured query plans, suitable indexing/search technology
and pagination. The inherited fixed-width overall site layout is unchanged.
Existing trip-detail links and HTML descriptions are outside this feature's scope.

 * Visible labels, focus outlines and field errors support keyboard navigation.

.search-field textarea { resize: vertical; }
.search-field [aria-invalid="true"] { border-color: #a12424; }
.field-error { display: block; color: #912020; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5; margin-top: 5px; }

05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/scripts/grant-staff.js
/**
 * One-time staff provisioning for the enhanced CS 465 database.
 * Dry run by default. --apply updates only an existing, exact-email account.
 */
require('dotenv').config();
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const User = require('../app_api/models/user');

async function main() {
  const email = process.argv.find(arg => arg.includes('@'))?.trim().toLowerCase();
  const apply = process.argv.includes('--apply');
  const databaseName = process.env.DB_NAME || 'travlr_capstone';
  if (!email || !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) throw new Error('Provide the exact existing account email.');
  if (databaseName === 'travlr') throw new Error('Refusing to change the original CS 465 database.');
  await mongoose.connect(`mongodb://${process.env.DB_HOST || '127.0.0.1'}/${databaseName}`);
  const user = await User.findOne({ email }).select('email role').exec();
  if (!user) throw new Error('No account with that email exists in the enhanced database.');
  if (!apply) {
    console.log(`Dry run: ${email} in ${databaseName} has role ${user.role || 'customer'}. Rerun with --apply to grant staff access.`);
    return;
  }
  await User.updateOne({ _id: user._id }, { $set: { role: 'staff' } });
  console.log(`Staff access granted to ${email} in ${databaseName}. Sign in again to refresh the admin navigation.`);
}

main().catch(error => { console.error(error.message); process.exitCode = 1; }).finally(() => mongoose.disconnect());
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/scripts/prepare-price-database.js
/**
 * CS 499 | Alondra Paulino Santos | 2026-09-17.
 * Copy the original local trips/users into a NEW capstone database. Convert only
 * the copied prices to Decimal128. Never write to the source or overwrite a target.
 * Dry-run by default; --apply makes the reviewed copy after a trip-data backup.
 */
require('dotenv').config({ quiet: true });
const fs = require('node:fs/promises');
const path = require('node:path');
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const { toDecimalPrice, canonicalPrice } = require('../app_api/utils/price');
const { BSON } = mongoose.mongo;

async function prepare({ apply = false, sourceName = 'travlr', targetName = process.env.DB_NAME || 'travlr_capstone' } = {}) {
  if (sourceName === targetName || targetName === 'travlr') {
    throw new Error('The enhanced database must be different from the original travlr database.');
  }
  const client = new mongoose.mongo.MongoClient(`mongodb://${process.env.DB_HOST || '127.0.0.1'}`, { serverSelectionTimeoutMS: 5000 });
  try {
    await client.connect();
    const source = client.db(sourceName);
    const target = client.db(targetName);
    if ((await target.listCollections().toArray()).length) {
      throw new Error('Target database is not empty. No changes made; do not overwrite an existing enhancement.');
    }
    const sourceCollections = (await source.listCollections().toArray()).map(c => c.name);
    if (!sourceCollections.includes('trips')) throw new Error('Source trips collection not found.');
    const trips = await source.collection('trips').find({}).toArray();
    if (!trips.length) throw new Error('Source has no trips. Confirm the source database before proceeding.');
    // Validate every amount before any database write. Fail rather than guess.
    const converted = trips.map(trip => {
      try { return { ...trip, perPerson: toDecimalPrice(trip.perPerson) }; }
      catch { throw new Error(`Trip ${trip.code || trip._id} has an invalid price; fix/review it before migration.`); }
    });
    const names = ['trips', 'users'].filter(name => sourceCollections.includes(name));
    const users = names.includes('users') ? await source.collection('users').find({}).toArray() : [];
    const indexes = {};
    for (const name of names) indexes[name] = await source.collection(name).indexes();
    console.log(`Validated ${trips.length} trip prices. ${apply ? 'Copying' : 'Dry run only:'} ${sourceName} -> ${targetName}.`);
    if (!apply) return;

    const backupDir = path.join(__dirname, '../backups');
    await fs.mkdir(backupDir, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
    const backupPath = path.join(backupDir, `original-trip-prices-${Date.now()}.json`);
    await fs.writeFile(backupPath, BSON.EJSON.stringify(trips, { relaxed: false }), { flag: 'wx', mode: 0o600 });
    // Original account hashes are copied unchanged so existing local logins work.
    // No account details, password hashes or connection credentials are logged.
    for (const name of names) {
      await target.createCollection(name);
      const records = name === 'trips' ? converted : users;
      if (records.length) await target.collection(name).insertMany(records, { ordered: true });
      for (const index of indexes[name]) {
        if (index.name === '_id_') continue;
        const { key, v, ns, ...options } = index;
        await target.collection(name).createIndex(key, options);
      }
    }
    const saved = await target.collection('trips').find({}).toArray();
    if (saved.length !== trips.length || saved.some((trip) => {
      const original = trips.find(t => t._id.equals(trip._id));
      return !(trip.perPerson instanceof mongoose.Types.Decimal128) || canonicalPrice(trip.perPerson) !== canonicalPrice(original.perPerson);
    })) throw new Error('Post-copy verification failed. Original database remains unchanged.');
    console.log(`Verified ${saved.length} decimal prices. Original database unchanged. Backup: ${backupPath}`);
  } finally {
    await client.close();
  }
}

if (require.main === module) {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  if (args.some(arg => arg !== '--apply')) {
    console.error('Usage: npm run prepare:prices -- [--apply]'); process.exitCode = 1;
  } else prepare({ apply: args.includes('--apply') }).catch(error => {
    console.error(error.message); process.exitCode = 1;
  });
}
module.exports = { prepare };
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/tests/inquiries.integration.js
/** Staff API checks use a disposable database, never the user's application data. */
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const { randomUUID, randomBytes } = require('node:crypto');
const { once } = require('node:events');
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const testDatabase = `cs465_staff_test_${randomUUID().replaceAll('-', '')}`;
process.env.DB_NAME = testDatabase;
process.env.DB_HOST = '127.0.0.1:27017';
process.env.JWT_SECRET = randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
let server;

async function run() {
  const connected = once(mongoose.connection, 'connected');
  const app = require('../app');
  await connected;
  const User = require('../app_api/models/user');
  const Inquiry = require('../app_api/models/inquiry');
  const staff = new User({ name: 'Staff', email: 'staff@example.test', role: 'staff' });
  staff.setPassword('test-only-password');
  await staff.save();
  const customer = new User({ name: 'Customer', email: 'customer@example.test' });
  customer.setPassword('test-only-password');
  await customer.save();
  const inquiries = await Inquiry.create(Array.from({ length: 21 }, (_, i) => ({
    tripCode: 'TEST', tripName: 'Test Trip', fullName: `Customer ${i}`,
    email: `customer${i}@example.test`, travelerCount: (i % 4) + 1,
    message: 'Please tell me about this trip.'
  })));
  server = app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
  await once(server, 'listening');
  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
  const request = (method, path, token, body) => fetch(`${base}${path}`, {
    method,
    headers: { ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}), ...(body ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}) },
    body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined
  });
  let response = await request('GET', '/api/inquiries');
  assert.equal(response.status, 401);
  response = await request('GET', '/api/inquiries', customer.generateJwt());
  assert.equal(response.status, 403);
  const staffToken = staff.generateJwt();
  response = await request('GET', '/api/inquiries?page=1', staffToken);
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  let page = await response.json();
  assert.equal(page.total, 21);
  assert.equal(page.items.length, 20);
  assert.equal(page.pageCount, 2);
  assert.equal(page.items[0].message, 'Please tell me about this trip.');
  assert.match(page.items[0].referenceNumber, /^INQ-\d{8}-[A-F0-9]{8}$/);
  assert.ok(Number.isInteger(page.items[0].travelerCount));
  response = await request('GET', '/api/inquiries?page=2', staffToken);
  assert.equal((await response.json()).items.length, 1);
  for (const path of ['/api/inquiries?page=0', '/api/inquiries?status=all', '/api/inquiries?page=1&page=2']) {
    response = await request('GET', path, staffToken);
    assert.equal(response.status, 400);
  }
  const id = inquiries[0]._id.toString();
  response = await request('PATCH', `/api/inquiries/${id}/status`, customer.generateJwt(), { status: 'closed' });
  assert.equal(response.status, 403);
  response = await request('PATCH', `/api/inquiries/${id}/status`, staffToken, { status: 'closed', email: 'changed@example.test' });
  assert.equal(response.status, 400);
  response = await request('PATCH', `/api/inquiries/${id}/status`, staffToken, { status: 'other' });
  assert.equal(response.status, 400);
  response = await request('PATCH', `/api/inquiries/${id}/status`, staffToken, { status: 'in_progress' });
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  assert.equal((await response.json()).status, 'in_progress');
  response = await request('GET', '/api/inquiries?status=in_progress', staffToken);
  page = await response.json();
  assert.equal(page.total, 1);
  assert.equal(page.items[0]._id, id);
  response = await request('PATCH', `/api/inquiries/${id}/status`, staffToken, { status: 'closed' });
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  assert.equal((await Inquiry.findById(id)).status, 'closed');
  response = await request('POST', '/api/register', undefined, { name: 'Self Register', email: 'self@example.test', password: 'test-only-password', role: 'staff' });
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  assert.equal((await User.findOne({ email: 'self@example.test' })).role, 'customer');
  // A token issued while staff must stop working as soon as its database role changes.
  await User.updateOne({ _id: staff._id }, { $set: { role: 'customer' } });
  response = await request('GET', '/api/inquiries', staffToken);
  assert.equal(response.status, 403);
  console.log('PASS: staff-only access, pagination/filtering, safe status updates, registration role, and role revocation.');
}

run().catch(error => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; }).finally(async () => {
  if (server) await new Promise(resolve => server.close(resolve));
  if (mongoose.connection.readyState === 1 && mongoose.connection.name === testDatabase && /^cs465_staff_test_[a-f0-9]{32}$/.test(testDatabase)) {
    await mongoose.connection.dropDatabase();
  }
  await mongoose.disconnect();
});
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/tests/inquiry.integration.js
/** Exercise the public inquiry flow in an isolated, disposable MongoDB database. */
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const { randomUUID, randomBytes } = require('node:crypto');
const { once } = require('node:events');
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const testDatabase = `cs465_inquiry_test_${randomUUID().replaceAll('-', '')}`;
process.env.DB_NAME = testDatabase;
process.env.DB_HOST = '127.0.0.1:27017';
process.env.JWT_SECRET = randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
let server;

async function run() {
  const connected = once(mongoose.connection, 'connected');
  const app = require('../app');
  await connected;
  const Trip = require('../app_api/models/travlr');
  const Inquiry = require('../app_api/models/inquiry');
  await Trip.create({ code: 'INQUIRY1', name: 'Reef Escape', length: '2 days', start: '2026-10-01', resort: 'Ocean', image: 'reef.jpg', description: 'A test trip', perPerson: 125.5 });
  server = app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
  await once(server, 'listening');
  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
  process.env.TRIPS_API_URL = `${base}/api/trips`;
  const path = '/travel/INQUIRY1/inquiry';
  let response = await fetch(`${base}/travel`);
  assert.match(await response.text(), /Ask about this trip/);
  response = await fetch(`${base}${path}`);
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  let html = await response.text();
  assert.match(html, /Reef Escape/);
  assert.match(html, /\$125\.50/);
  const token = html.match(/name="formToken" value="([^"]+)"/)?.[1];
  assert.ok(token);
  const submit = (fields, url = path) => fetch(`${base}${url}`, { method: 'POST', redirect: 'manual', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: new URLSearchParams(fields) });
  const inquiry = { formToken: token, fullName: '  María Rivera  ', email: '  MARIA@example.com  ', travelerCount: '2', message: '  Do you have a room for two?  ' };
  response = await submit({ ...inquiry, email: 'invalid', message: 'short' });
  assert.equal(response.status, 400);
  html = await response.text();
  assert.match(html, /Enter a valid email address/);
  assert.match(html, /value="María Rivera"/);
  assert.equal(await Inquiry.countDocuments(), 0);
  response = await submit({ ...inquiry, formToken: 'invalid' });
  assert.equal(response.status, 403);
  assert.equal(await Inquiry.countDocuments(), 0);
  response = await submit(inquiry, '/travel/WRONG/inquiry');
  assert.equal(response.status, 404);
  assert.equal(await Inquiry.countDocuments(), 0);
  response = await submit(inquiry);
  assert.equal(response.status, 303);
  const receiptPath = response.headers.get('location');
  assert.match(receiptPath, /^\/travel\/INQUIRY1\/inquiry\/sent\?receipt=/);
  const saved = await Inquiry.findOne({ tripCode: 'INQUIRY1' });
  assert.ok(saved);
  assert.equal(saved.tripName, 'Reef Escape');
  assert.equal(saved.fullName, 'María Rivera');
  assert.equal(saved.email, 'maria@example.com');
  assert.equal(saved.travelerCount, 2);
  assert.equal(saved.message, 'Do you have a room for two?');
  assert.equal(saved.status, 'new');
  assert.match(saved.referenceNumber, /^INQ-\d{8}-[A-F0-9]{8}$/);
  response = await fetch(`${base}${path}/sent`);
  assert.equal(response.status, 404);
  response = await fetch(`${base}${receiptPath}`);
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  html = await response.text();
  assert.match(html, /saved for review/);
  assert.match(html, new RegExp(saved.referenceNumber));
  assert.equal(await Inquiry.countDocuments(), 1);
  console.log('PASS: trip-linked form, validation, signed token, saved inquiry, staff status and redirect confirmation.');
}

run().catch(error => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; }).finally(async () => {
  if (server) await new Promise(resolve => server.close(resolve));
  if (mongoose.connection.readyState === 1 && mongoose.connection.name === testDatabase && /^cs465_inquiry_test_[a-f0-9]{32}$/.test(testDatabase)) {
    await mongoose.connection.dropDatabase();
  }
  await mongoose.disconnect();
});
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/tests/inquiry.test.js
/** Validate the saved fields independently of the browser controls. */
const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const { validateInquiry } = require('../app_server/utils/inquiry-validation');

test('trims valid inquiry values and accepts ordinary names, emails and questions', () => {
  const { values, errors } = validateInquiry({ fullName: '  María Rivera  ', email: '  Maria@example.com  ', travelerCount: ' 2 ', message: '  Do you have a room for two?  ' });
  assert.deepEqual(errors, {});
  assert.deepEqual(values, { fullName: 'María Rivera', email: 'Maria@example.com', travelerCount: '2', message: 'Do you have a room for two?' });
});

test('rejects missing, malformed and repeated values with field-specific errors', () => {
  for (const body of [
    {}, { fullName: ['One', 'Two'], email: 'a@example.com', travelerCount: '2', message: 'A question for you' },
    { fullName: 'A', email: 'not an email', travelerCount: '0', message: 'short' },
    { fullName: 'Normal Name', email: 'a@example.com', travelerCount: '21', message: 'x'.repeat(2001) },
    { fullName: 'Normal Name', email: 'a@example.com', travelerCount: '2.5', message: 'A question for you' }
  ]) {
    const result = validateInquiry(body);
    assert.ok(Object.keys(result.errors).length);
    assert.ok(result.values.fullName.length <= 100);
    assert.ok(result.values.message.length <= 2000);
  }
});
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/tests/price.integration.js
/** Local-only smoke test. Writes exclusively to a unique temporary test database. */
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const { randomUUID, randomBytes } = require('node:crypto');
const { once } = require('node:events');
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const testDatabase = `cs465_price_test_${randomUUID().replaceAll('-', '')}`;
process.env.DB_NAME = testDatabase;
process.env.DB_HOST = '127.0.0.1:27017';
process.env.JWT_SECRET = randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
let server;
const realFetch = global.fetch;

async function run() {
  const connected = once(mongoose.connection, 'connected');
  const app = require('../app');
  await connected;
  const Trip = require('../app_api/models/travlr');
  const User = require('../app_api/models/user');
  const staff = new User({ name: 'Price Test Staff', email: 'price-staff@example.test', role: 'staff' });
  staff.setPassword(randomBytes(16).toString('hex'));
  await staff.save();
  server = app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
  await once(server, 'listening');
  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
  // Route the public controller's internal API call to this test server only.
  process.env.TRIPS_API_URL = `${base}/api/trips`;
  const headers = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Authorization: `Bearer ${staff.generateJwt()}` };
  const sample = { code: 'PRICE_TEST', name: 'Price Test', length: '2 days', start: '2026-10-01', resort: 'Test', image: 'reef.jpg', description: 'Test', perPerson: 125.5 };
  const request = (method, url, body) => realFetch(`${base}${url}`, { method, headers, body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body) });

  let response = await request('POST', '/api/trips', sample);
  assert.equal(response.status, 201);
  assert.equal((await response.json()).perPerson, 125.5);
  let record = await mongoose.connection.db.collection('trips').findOne({ code: sample.code });
  assert.equal(record.perPerson._bsontype, 'Decimal128');
  assert.equal(record.perPerson.toString(), '125.50');
  response = await request('GET', `/api/trips/${sample.code}`);
  assert.equal((await response.json())[0].perPerson, 125.5);

  response = await request('PUT', `/api/trips/${sample.code}`, { ...sample, perPerson: 99.99 });
  assert.equal(response.status, 201);
  assert.equal((await response.json()).perPerson, 99.99);
  for (const price of [-1, '99.99', '', null, 1.001]) {
    response = await request('PUT', `/api/trips/${sample.code}`, { ...sample, perPerson: price });
    assert.equal(response.status, 400);
  }
  record = await mongoose.connection.db.collection('trips').findOne({ code: sample.code });
  assert.equal(record.perPerson.toString(), '99.99');
  response = await request('GET', '/travel');
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  assert.match(await response.text(), /\$99\.99 per person/);

  response = await request('PUT', `/api/trips/${sample.code}`, { ...sample, perPerson: 0 });
  assert.equal(response.status, 201);
  assert.equal((await response.json()).perPerson, 0);
  await Trip.create([{ ...sample, code: 'TWENTY', perPerson: 20 }, { ...sample, code: 'HUNDRED', perPerson: 100 }]);
  const sorted = await Trip.find({}).sort({ perPerson: 1 });
  assert.deepEqual(sorted.map(t => t.toJSON().perPerson), [0, 20, 100]);
  const filtered = await Trip.find({ perPerson: { $gte: mongoose.Types.Decimal128.fromString('20.00') } });
  assert.equal(filtered.length, 2);
  response = await request('DELETE', `/api/trips/${sample.code}`);
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  assert.equal((await response.json()).perPerson, 0);
  console.log('PASS: API create/read/update/delete, rejection, exact Decimal128 storage, numeric sorting/filtering, and public currency display.');
}

run().catch(error => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; }).finally(async () => {
  global.fetch = realFetch;
  if (server) await new Promise(resolve => server.close(resolve));
  // Only the randomly named database created by this run may be removed.
  if (mongoose.connection.readyState === 1 && mongoose.connection.name === testDatabase && /^cs465_price_test_[a-f0-9]{32}$/.test(testDatabase)) {
    await mongoose.connection.dropDatabase();
  }
  await mongoose.disconnect();
});
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/tests/price.test.js
const { test } = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const Trip = require('../app_api/models/travlr');
const { canonicalPrice, toDecimalPrice, isApiPrice, MAX_PRICE } = require('../app_api/utils/price');
const { formatPrice } = require('../app_server/utils/price-display');
const { tripsAddTrip, tripsUpdateTrip } = require('../app_api/controllers/trips');

const sample = { code: 'TEST', name: 'Test', length: '2 nights', start: '2026-10-01', resort: 'Test', image: 'reef.jpg', description: 'Test', perPerson: 19.99 };

test('valid prices retain dollars/cents and zero in Decimal128 and JSON', async () => {
  for (const price of [0, 0.01, 0.1, 19.99, 125, 799, 1199, 1999, 3499, MAX_PRICE]) {
    assert.equal(isApiPrice(price), true);
    const trip = new Trip({ ...sample, perPerson: price });
    await trip.validate();
    assert.ok(trip.perPerson instanceof mongoose.Types.Decimal128);
    assert.equal(trip.perPerson.toString(), canonicalPrice(price));
    assert.equal(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(trip)).perPerson, price);
  }
});

test('legacy plain decimal strings migrate without changing amounts', () => {
  for (const [input, expected] of [['799.00', '799.00'], [' 00125.5 ', '125.50'], ['0', '0.00']]) {
    assert.equal(toDecimalPrice(input).toString(), expected);
  }
});

test('invalid values are rejected, never silently rounded or converted to zero', async () => {
  for (const price of ['', ' ', '$799.00', '1,199.00', 'NaN', 'Infinity', '1e2', '0x20', '-1', '1.001', null, undefined, true, [], {}, -1, NaN, Infinity, 0.001, MAX_PRICE + 1]) {
    assert.throws(() => toDecimalPrice(price));
    assert.equal(isApiPrice(price), false);
    await assert.rejects(new Trip({ ...sample, perPerson: price }).validate());
  }
  assert.equal(isApiPrice('125.00'), false, 'API requires a number even though migration accepts legacy text');
});

test('hydrate Decimal128 records and serialize numeric prices', () => {
  const trip = Trip.hydrate({ ...sample, perPerson: toDecimalPrice(125.5) });
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(trip)).perPerson, 125.5);
});

test('display formatting keeps two cents digits and never displays NaN', () => {
  assert.equal(formatPrice(125), '$125.00');
  assert.equal(formatPrice(1199.5), '$1,199.50');
  assert.equal(formatPrice(0), '$0.00');
  assert.equal(formatPrice(undefined), 'Price unavailable');
});

test('both write controllers reject invalid/missing prices before database access', async () => {
  for (const controller of [tripsAddTrip, tripsUpdateTrip]) {
    for (const price of [undefined, null, '', '125', true, -1, 1.001, MAX_PRICE + 1]) {
      let status, body;
      const res = { status(value) { status = value; return this; }, json(value) { body = value; return this; } };
      await controller({ body: { ...sample, perPerson: price }, params: { tripCode: 'TEST' } }, res);
      assert.equal(status, 400);
      assert.match(body.message, /Price must/);
    }
  }
});
05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/tests/search.integration.js
/**
 * Search API + rendered-page regression checks against a disposable database.
 * Never write to the original or enhanced application databases.
 */
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const { randomUUID } = require('node:crypto');
const { once } = require('node:events');
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const testDatabase = `cs465_search_test_${randomUUID().replaceAll('-', '')}`;
process.env.DB_NAME = testDatabase;
process.env.DB_HOST = '127.0.0.1:27017';
let server;

async function run() {
  const connected = once(mongoose.connection, 'connected');
  const app = require('../app');
  await connected;
  const Trip = require('../app_api/models/travlr');
  const common = { length: '2 days', resort: 'Ocean', image: 'reef.jpg', description: 'Search test' };
  await Trip.create([
    { ...common, code: 'A', name: 'Reef Escape', perPerson: 100, start: '2026-10-01T23:59:59.999Z' },
    { ...common, code: 'B', name: 'Beach', resort: 'Reef Resort', perPerson: 20, start: '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z' },
    { ...common, code: 'C', name: 'Mountain', perPerson: 0, start: '2026-10-02T00:00:00Z' },
    { ...common, code: 'D', name: 'Literal .* trip', perPerson: 200, start: '2026-09-30T00:00:00Z' }
  ]);
  server = app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
  await once(server, 'listening');
  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
  process.env.TRIPS_API_URL = `${base}/api/trips`;
  async function codes(query) {
    const response = await fetch(`${base}/api/trips?${query}`);
    assert.equal(response.status, 200);
    return (await response.json()).map(trip => trip.code);
  }
  assert.deepEqual(await codes(''), ['D', 'B', 'A', 'C']);
  assert.deepEqual(await codes('q=REEF&minPrice=20&maxPrice=100&startDate=2026-10-01&endDate=2026-10-01&sort=priceDesc'), ['A', 'B']);
  assert.deepEqual(await codes('sort=priceAsc'), ['C', 'B', 'A', 'D']);
  assert.deepEqual(await codes('minPrice=0&maxPrice=0'), ['C']);
  assert.deepEqual(await codes('q=.*'), ['D']);
  assert.deepEqual(await codes('q=notfound'), []);
  for (const query of ['q=a&q=b', 'minPrice[$gt]=1', 'sort=invalid', 'startDate=2026-02-30', 'minPrice=100&maxPrice=20']) {
    const response = await fetch(`${base}/api/trips?${query}`);
    assert.equal(response.status, 400, query);
    assert.ok((await response.json()).errors);
  }
  let response = await fetch(`${base}/travel?q=Reef&maxPrice=20&sort=priceAsc`);
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  let html = await response.text();
  assert.match(html, /value="Reef"/);
  assert.match(html, /\$20\.00 per person/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(html, /\$100\.00 per person/);
  assert.match(html, /value="priceAsc" selected/);
  response = await fetch(`${base}/travel?q=notfound`);
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  assert.match(await response.text(), /No trips match/);
  response = await fetch(`${base}/travel?minPrice=100&maxPrice=20`);
  assert.equal(response.status, 400);
  assert.match(await response.text(), /Maximum price must be at least/);
  response = await fetch(`${base}/travel?q=${encodeURIComponent('<script>alert(1)</script>')}`);
  html = await response.text();
  assert.doesNotMatch(html, /<script>alert\(1\)<\/script>/);
  assert.match(html, /&lt;script&gt;/);

  // Simulate a failed query without stopping the user's MongoDB service.
  const originalFind = Trip.find;
  try {
    Trip.find = () => { throw new Error('PRIVATE_DATABASE_DETAILS'); };
    response = await fetch(`${base}/api/trips`);
    assert.equal(response.status, 503);
    assert.doesNotMatch(await response.text(), /PRIVATE_DATABASE_DETAILS/);
    response = await fetch(`${base}/travel`);
    assert.equal(response.status, 503);
    html = await response.text();
    assert.match(html, /Unable to retrieve trips/);
    assert.doesNotMatch(html, /PRIVATE_DATABASE_DETAILS/);
  } finally { Trip.find = originalFind; }
  console.log('PASS: combined filters, numeric sorting, inclusive dates, literal text, validation, retained form values, empty results, escaping and safe failure messages.');
}

run().catch(error => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; }).finally(async () => {
  if (server) await new Promise(resolve => server.close(resolve));
  if (mongoose.connection.readyState === 1 && mongoose.connection.name === testDatabase && /^cs465_search_test_[a-f0-9]{32}$/.test(testDatabase)) {
    await mongoose.connection.dropDatabase();
  }
  await mongoose.disconnect();
});

05-Code-Evidence/02-Enhanced-Source/tests/trip-search.test.js
/** Search contract regression tests: no database or customer data needed. */
const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const { buildTripSearch, SearchValidationError, SORT_OPTIONS } = require('../app_api/utils/trip-search');

test('empty search has no filters and uses deterministic departure order', () => {
  const result = buildTripSearch({ q: '  ', minPrice: '' });
  assert.deepEqual(result.filter, {});
  assert.deepEqual(result.sort, { start: 1, _id: 1 });
});

test('search trims text and treats regex characters literally, ignoring case', () => {
  const { filter, criteria } = buildTripSearch({ q: '  Reef.* [A]  ' });
  assert.equal(criteria.q, 'Reef.* [A]');
  assert.ok(filter.$or[0].name.test('reef.* [a] vacation'));
  assert.ok(!filter.$or[0].name.test('Reefanything A'));
  assert.ok(filter.$or[1].resort.test('REEF.* [A]'));
});

test('combined price and date bounds preserve zero and the entire end day', () => {
  const { filter } = buildTripSearch({ q: 'reef', minPrice: '0', maxPrice: '100.25', startDate: '2028-02-28', endDate: '2028-02-29' });
  assert.equal(filter.perPerson.$gte.toString(), '0.00');
  assert.equal(filter.perPerson.$lte.toString(), '100.25');
  assert.equal(filter.start.$gte.toISOString(), '2028-02-28T00:00:00.000Z');
  assert.equal(filter.start.$lt.toISOString(), '2028-03-01T00:00:00.000Z');
  assert.ok(filter.$or);
});

test('invalid inputs fail with field errors instead of becoming database queries', () => {
  const cases = [
    [{ q: 'a'.repeat(101) }, 'q'], [{ q: ['a', 'b'] }, 'q'],
    [{ minPrice: { $gt: 0 } }, 'minPrice'], [{ minPrice: '-1' }, 'minPrice'],
    [{ maxPrice: '1.001' }, 'maxPrice'], [{ maxPrice: 'NaN' }, 'maxPrice'],
    [{ maxPrice: '1000000000' }, 'maxPrice'], [{ minPrice: '10', maxPrice: '0' }, 'maxPrice'],
    [{ startDate: '2026-02-29' }, 'startDate'], [{ endDate: '2026-04-31' }, 'endDate'],
    [{ startDate: '2026-10-02', endDate: '2026-10-01' }, 'endDate'],
    [{ sort: '$where' }, 'sort'], [{ $where: 'anything' }, 'query']
  ];
  for (const [input, field] of cases) {
    assert.throws(() => buildTripSearch(input), error => error instanceof SearchValidationError && Boolean(error.errors[field]));
  }
});

test('every allowed sort includes a unique tie breaker and returns a fresh object', () => {
  for (const option of SORT_OPTIONS) {
    const result = buildTripSearch({ sort: option.value });
    assert.deepEqual(result.sort, option.order);
    assert.notEqual(result.sort, option.order);
    assert.equal(result.sort._id, 1);
  }
});
